SELECT c."CourseId", c."AverageRating", c."CreatedAt", c."DeletedAt", c."DeletedByUserId", c."Description", c."InstructorId", c."IsDeleted", c."Level", c."NeedsReanalysis", c."Price", c."PrimaryCategoryId", c."ReviewCount", c."Slug", c."Status", c."StudentCount", c."ThumbnailUrl", c."Title", c."UpdatedAt", s."CourseId", s."CategoryId", s."CategoryId0", s."Description", s."IsActive", s."Name", s."ParentCategoryId", s."Slug"
FROM "Courses" AS c
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT c0."CourseId", c0."CategoryId", c1."CategoryId" AS "CategoryId0", c1."Description", c1."IsActive", c1."Name", c1."ParentCategoryId", c1."Slug"
    FROM "CourseCategories" AS c0
    INNER JOIN "Categories" AS c1 ON c0."CategoryId" = c1."CategoryId"
) AS s ON c."CourseId" = s."CourseId"
WHERE c."ThumbnailUrl" IS NULL OR c."ThumbnailUrl" = '' OR c."ThumbnailUrl" LIKE '%picsum.photos%' OR c."ThumbnailUrl" LIKE '%via.placeholder.com%'
ORDER BY c."CourseId", s."CourseId", s."CategoryId"

SELECT c."Name", (
    SELECT count(*)::int
    FROM "CourseCategories" AS c0
    INNER JOIN "Courses" AS c1 ON c0."CourseId" = c1."CourseId"
    WHERE c."CategoryId" = c0."CategoryId" AND c1."Status" = 'Published') AS "Published", (
    SELECT count(*)::int
    FROM "CourseCategories" AS c2
    INNER JOIN "Courses" AS c3 ON c2."CourseId" = c3."CourseId"
    WHERE c."CategoryId" = c2."CategoryId" AND c3."Status" = 'Draft') AS "Draft", (
    SELECT count(*)::int
    FROM "CourseCategories" AS c4
    WHERE c."CategoryId" = c4."CategoryId") AS "Total"
FROM "Categories" AS c
ORDER BY c."Name"
